The 4 agents run concurrently and each one's completion arrives as a message in this conversation when it finishes — do NOT issue a separate blocking call to wait for them.\n285\t\n286\t**Once all 4 agents have reported completion, read each agent's output file (single message with 4 Read calls):**\n287\t```\n288\tRead tool:\n289\t  file_path: \"{outputFile from that agent's async_launched result}\"\n290\t```\n291\t\n292\t> Allow up to `workflow.subagent_timeout` for the slowest agent to finish before treating it as failed. The timeout is configurable via `workflow.subagent_timeout` in `.planning/config.json` (milliseconds). Default: 300000 (5 minutes). Increase for large codebases or slower models.\n293\t\n294\tEach output file contains that agent's completion confirmation. Parse the confirmation marker (see below) from the file contents.\n295\t\n296\t**Expected confirmation format from each agent:**\n297\t```\n298\t## Mapping Complete\n299\t\n300\t**Focus:** {focus}\n301\t**Documents written:**\n302\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC1}.md` ({N} lines)\n303\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC2}.md` ({N} lines)\n304\t\n305\tReady for orchestrator summary.\n306\t```\n307\t\n308\t**What you receive:** Just file paths and line counts. NOT document contents.\n309\t\n310\tIf any agent failed, note the failure and continue with successful documents.\n311\t\n312\tContinue to verify_output.\n313\t</step>\n314\t\n315\t<step name=\"sequential_mapping\" condition=\"Agent tool is NOT available (e.g. Antigravity, Codex)\">\n316\tWhen the `Agent` tool is unavailable, perform codebase mapping sequentially in the current context. This replaces `spawn_agents` and `collect_confirmations`.\n317\t\n318\t**IMPORTANT:** Do NOT use `browser_subagent`, `Explore`, or any browser-based tool. It runs on\n376\tevery mapping run, incremental (`--paths`) and full alike. `--files` narrows it\n377\tto the documents an Update run actually refreshed; `--paths` needs no narrowing\n378\tbecause all seven are regenerated, just scoped in content.\n379\t\n380\t**Why this is a shell step and not an instruction to the mapper.** The stamp is\n381\tthe only machine-readable freshness marker: the `verify codebase-drift` gate\n382\treads it to decide what to diff HEAD against. The human-readable markers the\n383\tmapper writes (`**Analysis Date:**`, `<!-- refreshed: ... -->`) are restamped\n384\tunconditionally on an Update run, so a mapper that decides its work is already\n385\tdone and rewrites only the dates still looks fresh to a human. Leaving the\n386\tmachine-readable stamp to the same agent reproduces exactly the failure the\n387\tstamp exists to detect. A shell step cannot be skipped by a confident agent.\n388\t\n389\tThe command is non-blocking: it emits `skipped` with a `reason` outside a git\n390\trepo or when no documents exist. Report `stamped` and `commit` in the summary\n391\tif any entry in `failed` is non-empty; otherwise continue silently.\n392\t\n393\tRun in this position, before `commit_codebase_map`, the stamp lands on\n394\tdocuments the mapper just wrote, so its markdown whitespace normalization is\n395\tfolded into the same commit. Running `stamp-codebase-map` by hand against an\n396\talready-committed map reflows that map's whitespace as a side effect.\n397\t\n398\tContinue to scan_for_secrets.\n399\t</step>\n400\t\n401\t<step name=\"scan_for_secrets\">\n402\t**CRITICAL SECURITY CHECK:** Scan output files for accidentally leaked secrets before committing.\n403\t\n404\tRun secret pattern detection:\n405\t\n406\t```bash\n407\t# Check for common API key patterns in generated docs\n408\tgrep -E '(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|sk_live_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|sk_test_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|gho_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|glpat-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+|AKIA[A-Z0-9]{16}|xox[baprs]-[a-zA-Z0-9-]+|[REDACTED:gsd-scan]|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.)' .planning/codebase/*.md 2>/dev/null && SECRETS_FOUND=true || SECRETS_FOUND=false\n```\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=true:**\n\n```\n⚠️  SECURITY ALERT: Potential secrets detected in codebase documents!\n\nFound patterns that look like API keys or tokens in:\n[show grep output]\n\nThis would expose credentials if committed.\n\n**Action required:**\n1. Review the flagged content above\n2. If these are real secrets, they must be removed before committing\n3. Consider adding sensitive files to Claude Code \"Deny\" permissions\n\nPausing before commit. Reply \"safe to proceed\" if the flagged content is not actually sensitive, or edit the files first.\n```\n\nWait for user confirmation before continuing to commit_codebase_map.\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=false:**\n\nContinue to commit_codebase_map.\n</step>\n\n<step name=\"commit_codebase_map\">\nCommit the codebase map:\n\n```bash\ngsd_run query commit \"docs: map existing codebase\" --files .planning/codebase/*.md\n```\n\nContinue to offer_next.\n</step>\n\n<step name=\"offer_next\">\nPresent completion summary and next steps.\n\n**Get line counts:**\n```bash\nwc -l .planning/codebase/*.md\n```\n\n**Output format:**\n\n```\nCodebase mapping complete.\n\nCreated .planning/codebase/:\n- STACK.md ([N] lines) - {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NTk1v3dzbQzer1pTv8jhbB","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Architecture\n2\t\n3\tStatus: Accepted\n4\t\n5\tThis document describes how the portfolio site is structured: layers, import rules, folder layout, data flow, the content model, the design-token pipeline and the animation layer. It is written so the scaffold can be built directly from it. Decisions with a longer rationale live in the [ADR log](adr/README.md).\n6\t\n7\t## Overview\n8\t\n9\tThe site is a static, zero-backend, single-page React app served from GitHub Pages. There is no router, no global store and no data fetching. The architecture comes down to four seams:\n10\t\n11\t1. Typed content: all copy lives in TypeScript modules checked against one schema.\n12\t2. CSS-variable design tokens: five role colors per theme drive every surface.\n13\t3. An internal animation layer: the only place the two animation libraries are imported.\n14\t4. A build and deploy pipeline: Vite build, GitHub Actions, GitHub Pages.\n15\t\n16\tEverything else is conventional React composition with props flowing down.\n17\t\n18\t## Layers\n19\t\n20\tDependencies point down only. A layer may import from layers below it, never above.\n21\t\n22\t```text\n23\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n24\t| app/        main.tsx, App.tsx, Providers, Page.tsx                    |\n25\t|             Page.tsx is the ONLY file that imports content data       |\n26\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n27\t| sections/   Hero, About, Experience, Projects, Skills, Education,     |\n28\t|             Contact                      (props in, JSX out)          |\n29\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n30\t| layout/     Container, Section, SiteHeader, MobileNav, Footer,        |\n31\t|             SkipLink                                                  |\n32\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n33\t| components/ ui/ (Button, Tag, Card, Heading, Logo, ThemeToggle, Icon) |\n34\t|             motion-primitives/ (vendored source, treated as owned)    |\n35\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n36\t| anim/       the internal animation layer: the only place that         |\n37\t|             imports motion/react and @react-spring/web               |\n38\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n39\t| hooks/  lib/                  | styles/ (tokens.css, base.css,       |\n40\t| (useActiveSection, cn, a11y)  |  index.css = tailwind + theme map)   |\n41\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n42\t| content/ (typed TS data)      assets/ (images, fonts via Fontsource) |\n43\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n44\tBuild and deploy: vite.config.ts, lint boundary rules, GitHub Actions\n45\t```\n46\t\n47\t## Import rules\n48\t\n49\tOnly src/anim/ and src/components/motion-primitives/ may import motion/react or @react-spring/web.\n50\t\n51\tOnly src/app/Page.tsx imports content data.\n52\t\n53\t| Importer | May import | May not import |\n54\t|----------|-----------|----------------|\n55\t| sections/* | layout, components/ui, the anim barrel (src/anim/index.ts), content types (not data) | motion/react, @react-spring/*, components/motion-primitives/* directly, content data |\n56\t| layout/*, components/ui/* | the anim barrel, lib, hooks | sections, content data |\n57\t| components/motion-primitives/* | motion/react, lib/cn | everything else (vendor code, kept out of lint autofix churn) |\n58\t| anim/* | motion/react, @react-spring/web, components/motion-primitives/*, the token mirror in styles | sections, layout |\n59\t| app/Page.tsx | content data, sections | any animation library or anim internals |\n60\t\n61\tThese rules are enforced by oxlint's `no-restricted-imports` rule with per-folder overrides in `.oxlintrc.json`, covering dynamic `import()` and re-exports as well, so a violation fails CI rather than relying on review. Unit tests under `tests/unit` may import the libraries for assertions. A gesture library is not adopted up front. It would be added to the anim row only if a react-spring drag cannot be built well otherwise, after a React 19 compatibility check.\n62\t\n63\t## Folder structure\n64\t\n65\t```text\n66\trepo root\n67\t|-- public/                  URL-stable files only: favicon set, og image,\n68\t|                            resume.pdf (a phone-number-free copy), robots.txt\n69\t|-- index.html               meta and social tags, inline theme-init script,\n70\t|                            noscript fallback content\n71\t|-- vite.config.ts           base from env, Tailwind plugin, imagetools, \"@\" alias\n72\t|-- .oxlintrc.json           import-boundary rules (see Import rules)\n73\t|-- .github/workflows/       ci.yml, deploy.yml\n74\t`-- src/\n75\t    |-- main.tsx\n76\t    |-- app/                 App.tsx, Page.tsx, Providers.tsx\n77\t    |-- sections/            one folder per section (Hero, About, Experience,\n78\t    |                        Projects, Skills, Education, Contact)\n79\t    |-- layout/              Container, Section, SiteHeader, MobileNav,\n80\t    |                        Footer, SkipLink\n81\t    |-- components/\n82\t    |   |-- ui/              Button, Tag, Card, Heading, Logo, ThemeToggle, Icon\n83\t    |   `-- motion-primitives/   vendored, patched locally, logged in an ADR\n84\t    |-- anim/\n85\t    |   |-- index.ts         public barrel: the only animation import path\n86\t    |   |-- MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx\n87\t    |   |-- useMotionPreference.ts\n88\t    |   |-- tokens.ts        springs, durations, eases in both libraries' formats\n89\t    |   |-- declarative/     motion-owned wrappers (Reveal, Stagger, Presence,\n90\t    |   |                    TextReveal, ScrollProgress, Morph)\n91\t    |   `-- physics/         react-spring-owned wrappers (PressSpring and other\n92\t    |                        pointer-driven, velocity-carrying helpers)\n93\t    |-- content/\n94\t    |   |-- schema.ts        every type\n95\t    |   |-- profile.ts, experience.ts, projects.ts, skills.ts,\n96\t    |   |   education.ts, contacts.ts     one module per section\n97\t    |   |-- images.ts        image registry\n98\t    |   `-- index.ts         assembles siteContent with satisfies\n99\t    |-- hooks/               useActiveSection, useMediaQuery, useHoverCapable\n100\t    |-- lib/                 cn.ts, date formatting, a11y helpers\n101\t    |-- styles/\n102\t    |   |-- index.css        tailwind import, dark variant, theme mapping\n103\t    |   |-- tokens.css       role tokens, derived tokens, light and dark blocks\n104\t    |   `-- base.css         resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guard, fonts\n105\t    `-- assets/images/       hashed by Vite\n106\t```\n107\t\n108\tThe animation folder is named anim/ rather than motion/ so it is never confused with the motion package or its motion/react import path.\n109\t\n110\t## Data flow\n111\t\n112\tBuild time:\n113\t\n114\t```text\n115\tcontent/*.ts --(typed, satisfies)--> app/Page --props--> sections --props--> layout, ui\n116\tassets/*     --(imagetools, hashed imports)--> image fields in content\n117\t```\n118\t\n119\tRuntime, all client-side with no network use beyond same-origin assets:\n120\t\n121\t- Theme: an inline script in the document head reads localStorage, falls back to prefers-color-scheme, and sets the data-theme attribute on the html element before first paint. The theme provider in React only changes the attribute and storage on a user toggle.\n122\t- Motion preference: the OS reduced-motion setting (plus an optional site override) feeds one hook, useMotionPreference, which configures both animation libraries and the wrapper components. See Reduced motion.\n123\t- Scroll-spy: an IntersectionObserver over the section ids drives useActiveSection, which highlights the current section in the navigation.\n124\t- Pointer and touch input drive react-spring values written straight to the DOM, with no React re-render per frame.\n125\t- Project detail: a click or tap sets local state in the Projects section, which opens an overlay with focus handling.\n126\t\n127\tThere is no global state library. Three small contexts exist: theme, motion preference and, optionally, the active section. Everything else is props.\n128\t\n129\t## Content model\n130\t\n131\tAll types live in src/content/schema.ts:\n132\t\n133\t- Profile: name, tagline, bio paragraphs, optional quote, photo (ImageAsset), contacts.\n134\t- ContactLink: kind (email, github, linkedin or instagram), label, href, whether it is external.\n135\t- ExperienceEntry: id, role, org, optional org URL, optional logo, dates (DateRange), location, bullets, optional highlights and stack.\n136\t- Project: id, title, context kind (personal, employer, course or hackathon) with a label, dates, stack, bullets, relatedExperienceId, featured, optional links and image.\n137\t- EducationEntry: id, degree, school, location, dates, gpa, optional track.\n138\t- SkillGroup: id, label, items as a string array.\n139\t- DateRange: ISO start and end (or present) plus a display label in one normalized format.\n140\t- ImageAsset: src, alt, width, height, optional srcset.\n141\t\n142\tRules:\n143\t\n144\t- Content modules use satisfies against the schema so a missing field fails typecheck.\n145\t- Ordering lives in the data (array order or a priority field), never in components.\n146\t- Copy is taken verbatim from the reviewed content inventory; any edit is recorded there with before and after, not made silently in code.\n147\t- The featured flag decides what appears first on mobile, so \"key info in seconds\" is a data decision.\n148\t- Content tests check that ids are unique, related ids resolve, every image has alt text, every external link is https, and dates parse in descending order.\n149\t- A phone number is not a field anywhere in the model, and must never appear in site copy, metadata or the served resume.\n150\t\n151\t## Design tokens\n152\t\n153\t- Exactly five role tokens per theme: text, background, primary, secondary, accent. Light and dark are authored separately, not inverted.\n154\t- Derived tokens (surface, border, muted text, link, focus ring and so on) are computed from the five roles with color-mix(in oklch, ...). A palette swap therefore edits one block and every derived value follows.\n155\t- The theme is selected by the data-theme attribute on the html element.\n156\t- Tailwind v4 is configured CSS-first: a custom dark variant is bound to [data-theme=dark] with @custom-variant, and an @theme inline block maps utilities to the CSS variables. There is no tailwind.config.js.\n157\t- No raw hex values in components. Components use semantic utilities only; a CI check greps for stray hex.\n158\t- A contrast test recomputes every text, UI and focus pair in both themes against WCAG 2.2 AA (4.5:1 for body text, 3:1 for large text and UI) and fails the build on any miss.\n159\t- Spring and duration values that JavaScript needs are mirrored in CSS variables, with src/anim/tokens.ts as the canonical source for springs.\n160\t\n161\t## Animation layer\n162\t\n163\tsrc/anim/index.ts is the only animation import path for the rest of the app. It exposes a handful of semantic components and hooks, not library primitives.\n164\t\n165\t| Interaction class | Owner |\n166\t|-------------------|-------|\n167\t| Mount and unmount, presence, exit animations | motion |\n168\t| Layout and shared-element transitions | motion |\n169\t| Scroll-linked values and in-view reveals | motion |\n170\t| Text and number effects, stagger, ready-made motion-primitives prefabs | motion |\n171\t| Velocity-carrying physics: press-squish, rail hover lift with neighbor shift, theme toggle thumb | react-spring |\n172\t| Color and hover transitions, native scroll-snap rail swipe | CSS |\n173\t| Desktop wheel smoothing only | Lenis |\n174\t\n175\tThe rule: one library animates any given element. Never both libraries on one element or one property, and no Tailwind transition utilities on an element a library animates. If a vendored primitive duplicates a physics wrapper, one implementation is chosen per interaction and the other stays unused.\n176\t\n177\tSpring and duration tokens are defined once in src/anim/tokens.ts, exported in both libraries' formats (motion's stiffness and damping, react-spring's tension and friction), so the two feel like one system. Fine-pointer effects such as tilt, magnetic pull and spotlight are gated by (hover: hover) and (pointer: fine); touch gets a tap or press equivalent, or nothing. Every wrapper reads the motion preference, renders an instant equivalent when reduced, forwards className, and never hides content in its initial CSS state.\n178\t\n179\t## Reduced motion\n180\t\n181\tOne preference source, useMotionPreference, combines the OS prefers-reduced-motion setting with an optional site override and feeds three consumers:\n182\t\n183\t- motion: the provider renders MotionConfig with its reducedMotion setting. This disables transform and layout animation but not opacity or color, so wrappers handle parallax, scroll-linked and autoplay effects explicitly.\n184\t- react-spring: the provider calls Globals.assign({ skipAnimation }) so every spring jumps to its target; the effect cleans up on change.\n185\t- CSS: a @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) guard in base.css shortens transitions and animations and keeps scroll behavior native.\n186\t\n187\tContent is visible by default and is never hidden until JavaScript runs. Hidden initial states are applied client-side inside wrappers, and a noscript rule guarantees visibility. Pressed, focused and selected states must read without motion (color, outline, shape). Lenis is off under reduced motion. The provider listens for changes to the media query so a live OS toggle takes effect without a reload.\n188\t\n189\t## Assets\n190\t\n191\t- Images live in src/assets/images/ and are imported as modules (hashed, long-cached). vite-imagetools produces AVIF and WebP variants and srcset with intrinsic width and height to prevent layout shift. Below-the-fold images lazy-load; the hero image is high priority.\n192\t- Fonts are self-hosted through Fontsource (variable, latin subset, font-display swap), at most two families. No third-party font CDN.\n193\t- Brand icons (GitHub, LinkedIn, Instagram, email) are inline SVG, because the UI icon set has no brand glyphs. See the ADR log.\n194\t- The resume PDF served from public/ is a phone-number-free copy of the source resume.\n195\t- Only images that are actually used migrate from the previous site, with EXIF metadata stripped. Template leftovers are left behind.\n196\t\n197\t## Build and deploy\n198\t\n199\tGitHub Actions builds the site and publishes it to GitHub Pages. The Vite base comes from the VITE_BASE environment variable: the project URL path (for example /aa-phan-portfolio-v2/) while staging, and / after the cutover to the user-site URL. Assets are module-imported so no absolute asset paths exist; anything under public/ that JavaScript references goes through import.meta.env.BASE_URL. CI also builds with the project-path base so a broken sub-path is caught before launch. Steps, settings, cutover and rollback are in the deploy runbook (deploy-runbook.md).\n200\t\n201\t## Quality gates\n202\t\n203\t| Gate | Command | Fails the build when |\n204\t|------|---------|----------------------|\n205\t| Typecheck | `tsc -b` | A content field is missing or a reference is wrong |\n206\t| Lint with import boundaries | oxlint, warnings denied | A section imports an animation library or content data |\n207\t| Format check | Prettier with Tailwind class sorting | A file is not formatted |\n208\t| Unit tests | Vitest: shell parity, theme logic, motion preference, gate negatives | Any test fails |\n209\t| Path-case check | `scripts/check-path-case.mjs`, run locally and on Linux CI | An import differs in case from the file on disk |\n210\t| Raw color check | `scripts/check-colors.mjs` | Source outside the token file and vendored primitives holds a raw hex, rgb, hsl, oklch or oklab value, or a Tailwind default palette utility |\n211\t| Build | `vite build` | The Vite build errors |\n212\t| Initial JS budget | `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs` | The module script and modulepreloads exceed 150 kB gzipped |\n213\t| Phone-number check | `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` over the built site | A served file holds a phone-shaped string or a tel: link |\n214\t| Token contrast test | Lands with the contrast gate | Any text, UI or focus pair is below AA in either theme |\n215\t| Smoke tests | Playwright in CI | The hero is missing with JavaScript off, the theme flashes, or content is hidden or a transform animation runs with reduced motion emulated |\n216\t| axe | Later gate | Serious or critical violations in light or dark |\n217\t| Mobile Lighthouse | Later gate | Targets set in the scaffold milestone are missed |\n218\t\n219\t`npm run verify` chains the first nine rows in that order, and both CI workflows call it.\n220\t\n221\t## Stack\n222\t\n223\tVerified 2026-10-08; re-verify at scaffold.\n224\t\n225\t| Technology | Version | Purpose |\n226\t|------------|---------|---------|\n227\t| Vite | ^8.3.4 | Build and dev server |\n228\t| React and React DOM | ^19.3.0 | UI |\n229\t| TypeScript | ~6.0.3 | Types |\n230\t| @vitejs/plugin-react | ^6.1.2 | React transform |\n231\t| Tailwind CSS and @tailwindcss/vite | ^4.3.3 | Styling, first-party Vite plugin |\n232\t| motion | ~14.0.0 | Declarative animation, imported from motion/react |\n233\t| @react-spring/web | ~10.1.2 | Spring physics |\n234\t| clsx | ^2.1.1 | Class composition |\n235\t| tailwind-merge | ^3.7.0 | Class conflict resolution |\n236\t| lucide-react | ^1.53.0 | UI icons |\n237\t| lenis | 1.3.x | Desktop wheel smoothing |\n238\t| vite-imagetools | ^12.0.1 | Image resize and format variants |\n239\t\n240\tNot used: the react-spring umbrella package, the react-spring v11 beta, raw framer-motion imports, shadcn init, tailwind.config.js, a client-side router, 3D or WebGL.\n241\t\n242\t## Decisions\n243\t\n244\tIndividual decisions, their status and the list of still-open decisions are tracked in the [ADR log](adr/README.md).\n245\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01KwiSrAVQ97b4FGQaPaHmF8","agent_id":"a1915f27f75901260","session_id":"06d26458-403d-4f6b-9de8-8af7ecc5ba95","uuid":"5a2d3507-b50c-49c0-a3b8-c95fd446799e","timestamp":"2026-11-20T07:24:43.997Z","tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_01NTk1v3dzbQzer1pTv8jhbB","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"rule"}}],"subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Scan codebase tech and architecture"}

A bare\n34\t// key such as {tel:true} in minified JS, which React DOM ships in its list of input\n35\t// types, is not a link and is not flagged.\n36\t//\n37\t// Separators between the digit groups, in any run: hyphen, dot, slash, space, tab, and the\n38\t// typographic dashes and spaces a word processor or resume produces (en and em dash,\n39\t// non-breaking hyphen, minus sign, no-break, narrow no-break, thin, figure and the other\n40\t// Unicode spaces). Used for runs with no separators at all, where a loose ten-digit\n69\t// pattern would also match hashes and numeric constants. A number in a JS string literal is caught by the `literal`\n92\t  // view, which ignores code outside strings.\n93\t  {\n94\t    label: 'ten-digit run',\n95\t    views: ['raw', 'text'],\n96\t    re: /(?<![0-9A-Za-z])\\d{10}(?![0-9A-Za-z])/g,\n97\t    only: MARKUP_FILES,\n98\t  },\n99\t  {\n100\t    label: 'eleven-digit run',\n101\t    views: ['raw', 'text'],\n102\t    re: /(?<![0-9A-Za-z])\\+?1[2-9]\\d{2}[2-9]\\d{6}(?![0-9A-Za-z])/g,\n103\t    only: MARKUP_FILES,\n104\t  },\n105\t  { label: 'unseparated number', views: ['literal'], re: new RegExp(NANP_RUN, 'g') },\n106\t]\n107\t\n108\t// The string literals of a JS or JSON file, kept at their byte offsets with everything else\n109\t// blanked to NUL, so a numeric constant, an identifier or a comment can never match. This is\n110\t// a small tokenizer, not a parser: comments and regex literals are skipped so an apostrophe in\n111\t// either does not shift which quotes open a string, and the `${...}` expressions inside a\n112\t// template literal count as code (minified bundles hold numeric constants there).\n113\tconst REGEX_AFTER = new Set([...'(,=:[!&|?{};+-*%<>~^'])\n114\tfunction literalView(text) {\n115\t  const n = text.length\n116\t  const view = Buffer.alloc(n)\n117\t  const keep = (from, to) => view.write(text.slice(from, to), from, 'latin1')\n118\t  const templateDepth = [] // one entry per open `${`: the `{` nesting inside it\n119\t  let i = 0\n120\t  let prev = '' // last non-space character of code, for telling a regex from a division\n121\t\n122\t  // i is just past an opening backtick or a closing brace of a `${...}`.\n123\t  const templateBody = () => {\n124\t    const from = i\n125\t    while (i < n) {\n126\t      if (text[i] === '\\\\') i += 2\n127\t      else if (text[i] === '`') {\n128\t        keep(from, i)\n129\t        i++\n130\t        return\n131\t      } else if (text[i] === '$' && text[i + 1] === '{') {\n132\t        keep(from, i)\n133\t        i += 2\n134\t        templateDepth.push(0)\n135\t        return\n136\t      } else i++\n137\t    }\n138\t    keep(from, n)\n139\t  }\n140\t\n141\t  while (i < n) {\n142\t    const c = text[i]\n143\t    const next = text[i + 1]\n144\t    if (c === '/' && next === '*') {\n145\t      const end = text.indexOf('*/', i + 2)\n146\t      i = end < 0 ? 2 : 1\n153\t      keep(from, i)\n154\t      i++\n155\t      prev = c\n156\t    } else if (c === '`') {\n157\t      i++\n158\t      templateBody()\n159\t      prev = c\n160\t    } else if (\n161\t      c === '/' &&\n162\t      (prev === '' ||\n163\t        REGEX_AFTER.has(prev) ||\n164\t        /\\b(?:return|typeof)\\s*$/.test(text.slice(i - 10, i)))\n165\t    ) {\n166\t      let inClass = false\n167\t      for (i++; i < n && text[i] !== '\\n'; i++) {\n168\t        if (text[i] === '\\\\') i++\n169\t        else if (text[i] === '[') inClass = true\n170\t        else if (text[i] === ']') inClass = false\n171\t        else if (text[i] === '/' && !inClass) break\n172\t      }\n173\t      i++\n174\t      prev = '/'\n175\t    } else {\n176\t      if (templateDepth.length > 0) {\n177\t        if (c === '{') templateDepth[templateDepth.length - 1]++\n178\t        else if (c === '}') {\n179\t          if (templateDepth[templateDepth.length - 1] === 0) {\n180\t            templateDepth.pop()\n181\t            i++\n182\t            templateBody()\n183\t            continue\n184\t          }\n185\t          templateDepth[templateDepth.length - 1]--\n186\t        }\n187\t      }\n188\t      if (c > ' ') prev = c\n189\t      i++\n190\t    }\n191\t  }\n192\t  return view.toString('latin1')\n193\t}\n194\t\n195\t// PDF text lives in content streams, usually Flate-compressed, so a raw byte scan of the\n196\t// file cannot see it. Each stream is inflated when it inflates (otherwise its raw bytes are\n197\t// taken as an uncompressed content stream), then the strings it draws (Tj and TJ operators)\n198\t// are pulled out so a number split across kerned runs still reads as one. The number shapes\n199\t// apply to that drawn text only. Stream bytes are never scanned for them: drawing operands\n200\t// are decimals and integers (a move to a three-decimal x and a four-digit y) that read as a\n201\t// separated number, and xref offsets are ten-digit runs. Stream bodies are blanked out of the raw scan for the same\n202\t// reason, so the raw scan still covers the PDF's own dictionaries (document info strings,\n203\t// URI actions) at their byte offsets. tel: links are checked in decoded streams too.\n204\t// Limit: text set in a font with a custom or CID encoding stores glyph ids, not characters,\n205\t// and is not readable without the font's ToUnicode map, and strings inside compressed object\n206\t// streams are not read as text. The resume PDF is also reviewed by hand for that reason.\n207\tfunction pdfStreams(buf) {\n208\t  const text = buf.toString('latin1')\n209\t  const streams = []\n210\t  const open = /stream\\r?\\n/g\n211\t  let m\n212\t  while ((m = open.exec(text)) !== null) {\n213\t    const start = m.index + m[0].length\n214\t    const end = text.indexOf('endstream', start)\n215\t    if (end < 0) break\n216\t    // Resume after \"endstream\", whose tail would otherwise match \"stream\" again.\n217\t    open.lastIndex = end + 'endstream'.length\n218\t    const slice = buf.subarray(start, end)\n219\t    let content = slice\n220\t    try {\n221\t      content = zlib.inflateSync(slice, { finishFlush: zlib.constants.Z_SYNC_FLUSH })\n222\t    } catch {\n223\t      // Not Flate (an image, a font program, plain content): read the bytes as they are.\n224\t    }\n225\t    streams.push({ start, end, content })\n226\t  }\n227\t  return streams\n228\t}\n229\t\n230\t// The PDF text with every stream body replaced by NULs, so offsets are unchanged.\n231\tfunction maskStreams(text, streams) {\n232\t  let out = ''\n233\t  let at = 0\n234\t  for (const { start, end } of streams) {\n235\t    out += text.slice(at, start) + '\\0'.repeat(end - start)\n236\t    at = end\n237\t  }\n238\t  return out + text.slice(at)\n239\t}\n240\t\n241\tfunction decodePdfLiteral(body) {\n242\t  return body.replace(/\\\\(?:([0-7]{1,3})|(\\r?\\n)|(.))/gs, (_all, oct, eol, ch) => {\n243\t    if (oct) return String.fromCharCode(parseInt(oct, 8) & 0xff)\n244\t    if (eol) return ''\n245\t    return { n: '\\n', r: '\\r', t: '\\t', b: '\\b', f: '\\f' }[ch] ?? ch\n246\t  })\n247\t}\n248\t\n249\tfunction decodePdfHex(body) {\n250\t  const hex = body.replace(/\\s+/g, '')\n251\t  const padded = hex.length % 2 ? `${hex}0` : hex\n252\t  const bytes = Buffer.from(padded, 'hex')\n253\t  // A byte order mark marks UTF-16BE text, whose dashes and spaces are real characters.\n254\t  if (bytes[0] === 0xfe && bytes[1] === 0xff) return bytes.subarray(2).swap16().toString('utf16le')\n255\t  // Otherwise dropping NULs reads two-byte text whose characters are plain ASCII.\n256\t  return bytes.toString('latin1').replaceAll('\\0', '')\n257\t}\n258\t\n259\tfunction pdfShownText(content) {\n260\t  const out = []\n261\t  const operand = Vite builds `dist/` (`vite.config.ts`), and GitHub Actions gates it and publishes to GitHub Pages (`.github/workflows/deploy.yml`).\n\n## Component Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility | File |\n|-----------|----------------|------|\n| Entry | Mounts React into `#root` under `StrictMode`, imports global CSS and Inter font | `src/main.tsx` |\n| App shell | Wraps everything in `Providers`, renders the fixed theme toggle header | `src/app/App.tsx` |\n| Providers | Composes context providers (currently only `MotionPreferenceProvider`) | `src/app/Providers.tsx` |\n| Page | Composes page sections into `<main id=\"main\">` | `src/app/Page.tsx` |\n| Hero | Name, tagline, resume download, mailto and `#contact` links | `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx` |\n| Section stubs | Empty placeholder sections for experience, projects, about, skills, education, contact | `src/sections/Stubs/SectionStubs.tsx` |\n| Section layout | Standard `<section>` with heading and `Container` | `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx` |\n| Theme logic | Read/apply/subscribe to `data-theme` and `localStorage` | `src/lib/theme.ts` |\n| Theme hook | React binding for the theme value | `src/lib/useTheme.ts` |\n| Motion preference | Reads `prefers-reduced-motion`, exposes `{ reduced }` | `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts` |\n| Motion provider | Feeds the one preference to `MotionConfig` and react-spring `Globals` | `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx` |\n| Animation barrel | The only public import path for animation | `src/anim/index.ts` |\n| TextReveal | Declarative text reveal wrapper | `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx` |\n| ThemeThumb | Spring-animated thumb for the toggle | `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx` |\n| Tokens (JS) | Spring and duration constants shared by both libraries | `src/anim/tokens.ts` |\n| Design tokens (CSS) | Role colors per theme, derived tokens | `src/styles/tokens.css` |\n| Tailwind entry | `@import 'tailwindcss'`, `@custom-variant dark`, `@theme inline` mapping | `src/styles/index.css` |\n| Base CSS | Resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guards | `src/styles/base.css` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Single-page React app with flat composition. Props flow downward, there is no router and no global state store. Two React contexts exist (motion preference and, via the DOM, theme).\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- Static output only: `npm run build` produces `dist/`; no runtime server or API calls.\n- The DOM is the source of truth for theme: `html[data-theme]` is read by `readTheme()` and written by `applyTheme()` and the pre-paint script in `index.html`.\n- Animation is isolated behind one barrel (`src/anim/index.ts`). Lint rules enforce that sections and layout never import `motion` or `@react-spring/web` directly (`.oxlintrc.json`).\n- Static HTML in `index.html` mirrors the rendered Hero and section headings so content exists before JavaScript runs. `tests/unit/hero-parity.test.tsx` checks the Hero against that markup.\n- Content is inline JSX: copy lives in the components (e.g. `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`). The typed `src/content/` layer described in `docs/architecture.md` does not exist yet.\n\n## Layers\n\n**App shell (`src/app/`):**\n- Purpose: root composition and providers\n- Location: `src/app/`\n- Contains: `App.tsx`, `Providers.tsx`, `Page.tsx`\n- Depends on: `src/anim` (via `Providers`), `src/sections`, `src/components/ui`\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx`\n\n**Sections (`src/sections/`):**\n- Purpose: page content blocks\n- Location: `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx` (Hero) and `src/sections/Stubs/`\n- Contains: section components taking no props today\n- Depends on: `src/layout`, `src/anim` barrel (Hero imports `TextReveal`)\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**Layout (`src/layout/`):**\n- Purpose: shared page structure\n- Location: `src/layout/`\n- Contains: `Section.tsx` (props: `id`, `title`, `className`, `children`), `Container.tsx`\n- Depends on: `src/lib/utils.ts`\n- Used by: sections\n\n**UI components (`src/components/ui/`):**\n- Purpose: reusable interactive pieces\n- Location: `src/components/ui/`\n- Contains: `ThemeToggle.tsx` only. `Button`, `Tag`, `Card`, `Heading`, `Logo`, `Icon` are planned in `docs/architecture.md`.\n- Depends on: `src/anim` barrel, `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/lib/useTheme.ts`\n- Used by: `src/app/App.tsx`, sections (planned)\n\n**Animation (`src/anim/`):**\n- Purpose: the only layer that imports `motion/react` and `@react-spring/web`\n- Location: `src/anim/`\n- Contains: `index.ts` (barrel), `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `tokens.ts`, `declarative/TextReveal.tsx`, `physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`\n- Used by: `src/app/Providers.tsx`, `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`, `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`\n- Must not import: sections, layout, content (enforced by the `**/anim/**` override in `.oxlintrc.json`)\n\n**Lib and hooks (`src/lib/`):**\n- Purpose: framework-agnostic helpers and the theme hook\n- Location: `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/lib/useTheme.ts`, `src/lib/utils.ts`\n- Contains: theme read/apply/subscribe, `cn` class merge helper\n- Used by: components, `src/lib/useTheme.ts` wraps `theme.ts` for React\n\n**Styles (`src/styles/`):**\n- Purpose: design tokens and global CSS\n- Location: `src/styles/index.css` (entry, imported by `src/main.tsx`), `tokens.css`, `base.css`\n- Contains: Tailwind v4 config in CSS (`@theme inline`, `@custom-variant dark`), `@source` directives for `index.html` and `src/`\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (page load)\n\n1. Browser loads `index.html`. The inline script in `<head>` reads `localStorage.theme`, falls back to `prefers-color-scheme`, and sets `data-theme` on `<html>` before first paint (`index.html`, lines 9-24).\n2. Vite-built module `src/main.tsx` runs, imports `src/styles/index.css` and the Inter font, and calls `createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(...)` (`src/main.tsx`).\n3. `App` renders `Providers` (which renders `MotionPreferenceProvider`), then the fixed `ThemeToggle` header, then `Page` (`src/app/App.tsx`, `src/app/Providers.tsx`).\n4. `MotionPreferenceProvider` calls `useMotionPreference()`, sets react-spring `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation })`, and wraps children in `MotionConfig` (`src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`).\n5. `Page` renders `<main id=\"main\">` with `Hero` and `SectionStubs` (`src/app/Page.tsx`).\n\n### Theme Toggle Path\n\n1. User clicks the button in `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`. `toggle()` computes the next theme and calls `applyTheme(next)`.\n2. `applyTheme` sets `html[data-theme]` first, so the switch always works, then writes `localStorage` in a try block (`src/lib/theme.ts`).\n3. `useTheme` (`src/lib/useTheme.ts`) observes the attribute via `subscribeTheme`, so React re-renders the `aria-pressed` state.\n4. `ThemeThumb` (`src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`) animates the thumb with react-spring.\n\n### Cross-Tab and OS Theme Sync\n\n1. `subscribeTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts` listens to `storage` events and to `prefers-color-scheme` `change` events.\n2. When no stored choice exists, OS changes are followed. The attribute remains the single source of truth.\n\n### Motion Preference Path\n\n1. `useMotionPreference` (`src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`) queries `(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)`.\n2. `MotionPreferenceProvider` maps that to `MotionConfig reducedMotion` (`'always'` or `'never'`) and to react-spring `skipAnimation`.\n3. Base CSS applies reduced-motion guards (`src/styles/base.css`, `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)`).\n\n**State Management:**\n- No store library. Theme state lives in `html[data-theme]` plus `localStorage`. Motion preference is derived from `matchMedia`. Other UI state is local component state.\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**Animation barrel:**\n- Purpose: one import path for all motion, so libraries can be swapped without touching sections\n- Examples: `src/anim/index.ts`\n- Pattern: re-export of semantic components and hooks. Current exports: `ThemeThumb`, `MotionPreferenceProvider`, `useMotionPreference`, `TextReveal`, `springs`, `durations`, `easeOut`.\n- Usage: `import { TextReveal } from '@/anim'` (see `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`)\n\n**Theme API:**\n- Purpose: one module owns `data-theme` and storage\n- Examples: `src/lib/theme.ts` exports `Theme`, `THEME_STORAGE_KEY`, `readTheme`, `applyTheme`, `subscribeTheme`\n- Pattern: DOM attribute as state, React hook as a subscriber (`src/lib/useTheme.ts`)\n\n**Path alias:**\n- `@/` maps to `src/` in `vite.config.ts`, `vitest.config.ts`, and `tsconfig.app.json` (`paths`)\n- Use `@/sections/Hero/Hero`, not relative `../../` paths across layers\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**Browser entry:**\n- Location: `index.html` (loads `/src/main.tsx` as a module script)\n- Triggers: any page load of the deployed site or `npm run dev`\n- Responsibilities: pre-paint theme script, static fallback markup, module bootstrap\n\n**React root:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: module evaluation\n- Responsibilities: mount `<App />` into `#root`, global CSS import\n\n**Build entry:**\n- Location: `vite.config.ts`\n- Triggers: `npm run build`, `npm run dev`, `npm run preview`\n- Responsibilities: base path from `VITE_BASE`, plugins (`react()`, `tailwindcss()`), `@` alias\n\n**Quality gate entry:**\n- Location: `package.json` `verify` script, invoked by `.github/workflows/ci.yml` and `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`\n- Responsibilities: typecheck, lint, format, unit tests, source gates, build, dist gates\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers in `src/`.\n- **Global state:** `document.documentElement` `data-theme` attribute and `localStorage` key `theme` (`src/lib/theme.ts`). Module-level `Globals` from `@react-spring/web` is mutated by `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`.\n- **Circular imports:** None detected in the current `src/` tree.\n- **Static output:** No server code. Anything served must exist in `public/` or be imported as a module so Vite hashes it.\n- **Base path:** Absolute paths break under `VITE_BASE`. Use `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` (see `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`) or `%BASE_URL%` in `index.html`.\n- **Content rule:** A phone number must never appear in served output. `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` enforces this over `dist/`.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Hard-coded copy in index.html and components\n\n**What happens:** The Hero copy and the mailto address appear in both `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx` and the static markup in `index.html`.\n**Why it's wrong:** Edits to one copy drift from the other. `tests/unit/hero-parity.test.tsx` only catches drift that it checks.\n**Do this instead:** Change the component and the pre-rendered markup together, and keep `tests/unit/hero-parity.test.tsx` passing.\n\n### Importing animation libraries outside src/anim\n\n**What happens:** Nothing in `src/` does this today. The pattern to avoid is `import { motion } from 'motion/react'` inside `src/sections/` or `src/layout/`.\n**Why it's wrong:** `oxlint` fails the build (`no-restricted-imports` in `.oxlintrc.json`), and the animation barrel is then bypassed.\n**Do this instead:** Add a semantic wrapper in `src/anim/declarative/` or `src/anim/physics/`, export it from `src/anim/index.ts`, then import from `@/anim`.\n\n### Writing theme state without applyTheme\n\n**What happens:** Setting `data-theme` directly, or calling `localStorage.setItem('theme', ...)` from a component.\n**Why it's wrong:** The attribute and the stored value drift, and `subscribeTheme` treats the attribute as the source of truth.\n**Do this instead:** Call `applyTheme(next)` from `src/lib/theme.ts`.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Defensive fallbacks, no thrown errors in the app path.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- `localStorage` access is wrapped in try/catch (`src/lib/theme.ts` `applyTheme`, `storedTheme`; `index.html` inline script). Blocked storage falls back to in-memory choice.\n- `window.matchMedia` access is wrapped in try/catch with a `light` fallback (`src/lib/theme.ts` `systemTheme`, `subscribeTheme`).\n- `document.getElementById('root')!` in `src/main.tsx` assumes the root exists; there is no runtime guard.\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None in app code. Build scripts print to stdout or stderr (`scripts/check-no-phone.mjs`).\n**Validation:** Typecheck via `tsc -b` with `strict`, `noUnusedLocals`, `noUnusedParameters` (`tsconfig.app.json`). Runtime validation of theme values uses `isTheme` (`src/lib/theme.ts`).\n**Authentication:** Not applicable. No user accounts.\n**Accessibility:** Skip-free layout uses `<section aria-labelledby>` per section (`src/layout/Section.tsx`). The theme toggle exposes `aria-pressed` and `aria-label` (`src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`).\n\n## Planned but Not Yet Present\n\nThese are described in `docs/architecture.md` and do not exist in the repo as of this analysis. Plan against the tracked files, and verify before depending on them:\n- `src/content/` (typed content schema and per-section data modules). Copy currently lives in components.\n- `src/hooks/` (`useActiveSection`, `useMediaQuery`, `useHoverCapable`). The only hook present is `src/lib/useTheme.ts`.\n- `src/components/motion-primitives/` (vendored primitives). `tests/fixtures/motion-primitives/Ok.tsx` exists for lint tests only.\n- `src/assets/images/` and vite-imagetools image pipeline.\n- Section components beyond Hero (About, Experience, Projects, Skills, Education, Contact). They exist only as stubs.\n- Lenis smooth scroll, lucide-react icons.\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-20*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3290,"cache_read_input_tokens":73324,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3290,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01KwiSrAVQ97b4FGQaPaHmF8","agent_id":"a1915f27f75901260","session_id":"06d26458-403d-4f6b-9de8-8af7ecc5ba95","uuid":"b094d404-7b40-4502-8481-320b20dac80e","timestamp":"2026-11-20T07:26:48.629Z","request_id":"req_011ChDnN1q3eL3VwU8aoevAr","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Scan codebase tech and architecture"}

phases/, research/, codebase/\n├── docs/                  # architecture.md, deploy-runbook.md, contrast-matrix.md, adr/\n├── public/                # URL-stable static files: favicon.svg, resume.pdf\n├── scripts/               # build-time gates (.mjs) + scripts/lib/ + contrast-pairs.json\n├── src/                   # application source (see below)\n├── tests/                 # Vitest unit tests, Playwright e2e, shared fixtures\n├── index.html             # Vite HTML entry + pre-paint theme script + static fallback markup\n├── vite.config.ts         # Vite build/dev config\n├── vitest.config.ts       # Vitest config (jsdom)\n├── playwright.config.ts   # Playwright config (Chromium, preview on :4173)\n├── tsconfig*.json         # TypeScript project references (app, node, scripts, test)\n├── package.json           # npm scripts and dependencies\n├── package-lock.json      # npm lockfile\n├── DESIGN.md              # design notes\n├── .oxlintrc.json         # oxlint rules incl. import-boundary rules\n├── .oxlintignore          # oxlint ignore paths\n├── prettier.config.mjs    # Prettier config\n└── .prettierignore        # Prettier ignore paths\n```\n\nGenerated and not committed: `node_modules/`, `dist/`, `test-results/`, `playwright-report/`, `coverage/` (see `.gitignore`).\n\n## Directory Purposes\n\n**`src/app/`:**\n- Purpose: root composition\n- Contains: `App.tsx` (shell and fixed toggle header), `Providers.tsx` (context providers), `Page.tsx` (section order)\n- Key files: `src/app/App.tsx`, `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**`src/sections/`:**\n- Purpose: page-level content blocks, one folder per section\n- Contains: `Hero/Hero.tsx` (implemented), `Stubs/SectionStubs.tsx` (six empty sections: experience, projects, about, skills, education, contact)\n- Key files: `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`, `src/sections/Stubs/SectionStubs.tsx`\n\n**`src/layout/`:**\n- Purpose: shared structural components\n- Contains: `Section.tsx`, `Container.tsx`\n- Key files: `src/layout/Section.tsx`, `src/layout/Container.tsx`\n\n**`src/components/ui/`:**\n- Purpose: reusable UI controls\n- Contains: `ThemeToggle.tsx` (only file)\n- Key files: `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`\n\n**`src/anim/`:**\n- Purpose: the only home of animation library imports\n- Contains: `index.ts` (public barrel), `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `tokens.ts`, `declarative/TextReveal.tsx` (motion-owned), `physics/ThemeThumb.tsx` (react-spring-owned)\n- Key files: `src/anim/index.ts`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`\n\n**`src/lib/`:**\n- Purpose: non-React helpers and the theme hook\n- Contains: `theme.ts`, `useTheme.ts`, `utils.ts` (`cn`)\n- Key files: `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/lib/useTheme.ts`\n\n**`src/styles/`:**\n- Purpose: global CSS and design tokens\n- Contains: `index.css` (Tailwind entry, custom dark variant, `@theme inline` mapping), `tokens.css` (role colors, light and dark blocks), `base.css` (resets, focus, reduced-motion guards)\n- Key files: `src/styles/index.css`, `src/styles/tokens.css`\n\n**`src/` root files:**\n- `src/main.tsx` - React entry point\n\n**`public/`:**\n- Purpose: files referenced by URL and not imported by JS\n- Contains: `favicon.svg`, `resume.pdf`\n- Reference with `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` or `%BASE_URL%` (see `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`, `index.html`)\n\n**`scripts/`:**\n- Purpose: quality gates run by `npm run verify`\n- Contains: `check-no-phone.mjs`, `check-js-budget.mjs`, `check-colors.mjs`, `check-contrast.mjs`, `check-path-case.mjs`, `contrast-pairs.json`, `lib/color.mjs`, `lib/tokens.mjs`\n- Key files: `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs`, `scripts/lib/tokens.mjs`\n\n**`tests/`:**\n- Purpose: all test code outside `src/`\n- Contains:\n  - `tests/unit/` - Vitest suites: `boundaries.test.ts`, `gates.test.ts`, `hero-parity.test.tsx`, `layout.test.tsx`, `motion-preference.test.tsx`, `theme-script.test.ts`, `tokens.test.ts`\n  - `tests/e2e/` - Playwright specs: `smoke.spec.ts`, `motion.spec.ts`\n  - `tests/fixtures/` - sample source files used by tests (`anim/Imports.tsx`, `app/Page.tsx`, `motion-primitives/Ok.tsx`, `sections/Imports.tsx`)\n  - `tests/setup.ts` - Vitest setup file\n- Key files: `tests/unit/boundaries.test.ts`, `tests/unit/theme-script.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/smoke.spec.ts`\n\n**`docs/`:**\n- Purpose: project documentation\n- Contains: `architecture.md`, `deploy-runbook.md`, `contrast-matrix.md`, `adr/0001` through `adr/0008` plus `adr/README.md`\n- Key files: `docs/architecture.md`, `docs/adr/README.md`\n\n**`.planning/`:**\n- Purpose: GSD planning artifacts (not part of the build)\n- Contains: `PROJECT.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, Points:**\n- `index.html`: HTML entry and pre-paint theme script\n- `src/main.tsx`: React root\n- `src/app/App.tsx`: app shell\n\n**Configuration:**\n- `vite.config.ts`: base path, plugins, alias\n- `vitest.config.ts`: unit test config\n- `playwright.config.ts`: e2e config\n- `tsconfig.app.json`: app compiler options and `@/*` path\n- `.oxlintrc.json`: lint and import-boundary rules\n- `prettier.config.mjs`: formatting rules\n- `package.json`: scripts (`verify` is the full gate)\n\n**Core Logic:**\n- `src/lib/theme.ts`: theme state\n- `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`: motion configuration\n- `src/app/Page.tsx`: section order\n\n**Styling:**\n- `src/styles/tokens.css`: color roles and derived tokens\n- `src/styles/index.css`: Tailwind entry and theme mapping\n- `src/styles/base.css`: base rules\n\n**Testing:**\n- `tests/unit/`: unit tests\n- `tests/e2e/`: browser tests\n- `tests/fixtures/`: test inputs\n\n## Naming Conventions\n\n**Files:**\n- React components: PascalCase `.tsx` matching the export, e.g. `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx` exports `Hero`, `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx` exports `ThemeToggle`\n- Hooks: camelCase with `use` prefix in `.ts`, e.g. `src/lib/useTheme.ts`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`\n- Non-component modules: camelCase `.ts`, e.g. `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`\n- Scripts: kebab-case `.mjs`, e.g. `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs`\n- Tests: `*.test.ts` or `*.test.tsx` under `tests/unit/` (kebab-case names, e.g. `hero-parity.test.tsx`); `*.spec.ts` under `tests/e2e/`\n- Docs: UPPERCASE for planning docs (`PROJECT.md`), `docs/architecture.md` lowercase, ADRs as `NNNN-kebab-title.md`\n\n**Directories:**\n- Lowercase: `src/lib/`, `src/anim/`, `src/styles/`\n- Feature folders for sections use PascalCase: `src/sections/Hero/`, `src/sections/Stubs/`\n- Sub-role folders inside `anim/` use lowercase: `declarative/`, `physics/`\n\n**Exports:**\n- Section and component modules use named exports (`export function Hero()`), not default exports. Exception: `vite.config.ts`, `vitest.config.ts`, `playwright.config.ts` use default exports as config requires.\n- Shared helpers are named exports from `src/lib/` and `src/anim/tokens.ts`.\n\n**Imports:**\n- Use the `@/` alias for anything under `src/` (for example `import { TextReveal } from '@/anim'`). Relative imports are used only within a folder.\n\n## Where to Add New Code\n\n**New Section (e.g. About, Projects):**\n- Implementation: `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx` (named export, props typed inline)\n- Replace the matching stub in `src/sections/Stubs/SectionStubs.tsx`, and add the component to `src/app/Page.tsx` in the desired order\n- Use `Section` from `src/layout/Section.tsx` for the outer `<section>` and heading\n- Matching static markup for `index.html`: keep in step with the component (see `ARCHITECTURE.md`, Anti-Patterns)\n- Tests: `tests/unit/<name>.test.tsx`\n\n**New Reusable UI Component:**\n- Implementation: `src/components/ui/<Name>.tsx`\n- Tests: `tests/unit/<name>.test.tsx`\n\n**New Layout Primitive:**\n- Implementation: `src/layout/<Name>.tsx`\n\n**New Animation Wrapper:**\n- Motion-owned: `src/anim/declarative/<Name>.tsx`\n- react-spring-owned: `src/anim/physics/<Name>.tsx`\n- Export from `src/anim/index.ts`. Import from `@/anim` elsewhere. Never import `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` outside `src/anim/` (lint fails).\n- Shared spring and duration values go in `src/anim/tokens.ts`.\n\n**New Hook:**\n- Shared React hook: `src/lib/use<Name>.ts` (the planned `src/hooks/` folder does not exist yet; creating it is acceptable if more than two hooks are added)\n\n**New Utility:**\n- Pure helper: `src/lib/<name>.ts`\n- Class-name helper: `cn` already exists in `src/lib/utils.ts`\n\n**New Static Asset:**\n- Imported by a component (hashed by Vite): `src/assets/images/` (folder not yet created) or any `src/` path\n- Linked by URL only: `public/`, referenced with `import.meta.env.BASE_URL`\n\n**New Design Token:**\n- Color role or derived color: `src/styles/tokens.css` (both light and dark blocks)\n- Tailwind utility mapping: `src/styles/index.css` (`@theme inline`)\n- Raw colors are forbidden in components; `scripts/check-colors.mjs` enforces this\n\n**New Test Fixture:**\n- `tests/fixtures/<area>/`\n\n**New Build Gate:**\n- `scripts/check-<name>.mjs`, add an npm script in `package.json`, and add it to the `verify` chain\n\n**New Documentation Decision:**\n- `docs/adr/` with the next free number, indexed in `docs/adr/README.md`\n\n## Special Directories\n\n**`node_modules/`:**\n- Purpose: npm dependencies\n- Generated: Yes (`npm ci`)\n- Committed: No\n\n**`dist/`:**\n- Purpose: Vite build output, scanned by `check-js-budget` and `check-no-phone`, uploaded by the Pages workflow\n- Generated: Yes (`npm run build`)\n- Committed: No (`.gitignore`)\n\n**`test-results/`, `playwright-report/`, `playwright/.cache/`:**\n- Purpose: Playwright outputs\n- Generated: Yes\n- Committed: No (`.gitignore`)\n\n**`node_modules/.tmp/`:**\n- Purpose: TypeScript incremental build info (`tsBuildInfoFile` in `tsconfig.app.json`)\n- Generated: Yes\n- Committed: No\n\n**`.planning/`:**\n- Purpose: GSD planning documents and phase records\n- Generated: Partly (by planning tools)\n- Committed: Yes, tracked in git\n\n**`.planning/codebase/`:**\n- Purpose: this codebase map (produced by `/gsd-map-codebase`)\n- Generated: Yes\n- Committed: Browser: static SPA (no server)                   │\n│  index.html  ->  src/main.tsx  ->  <App/>                         │\n├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│ app/             ▼\n  localStorage (\"theme\")          html[data-theme] attribute\n  (`src/lib/theme.ts`)            (set by inline script in `index.html`)\n```\n\nBuild and delivery sit outside the app: Vite builds `dist/` (`vite.config.ts`), and GitHub Actions gates it and publishes to GitHub Pages (`.github/workflows/deploy.yml`).\n\n## Component Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility | File |\n|-----------|----------------|------|\n| Entry | Mounts React into `#root` under `StrictMode`, imports global CSS and Inter font | `src/main.tsx` |\n| App shell | Wraps everything in `Providers`, renders the fixed theme toggle header | `src/app/App.tsx` |\n| Providers | Composes context providers (currently only `MotionPreferenceProvider`) | `src/app/Providers.tsx` |\n| Page | Composes page sections into `<main id=\"main\">` | `src/app/Page.tsx` |\n| Hero | Name, tagline, resume download, mailto and `#contact` links | `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx` |\n| Section stubs | Empty placeholder sections for experience, projects, about, skills, education, contact | `src/sections/Stubs/SectionStubs.tsx` |\n| Section layout | Standard `<section>` with heading and `Container` | `src/layout/Section.tsx` |\n| Container | Max-width and padding wrapper | `src/layout/Container.tsx` |\n| ThemeToggle | Button that flips light/dark via `applyTheme` | `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx` |\n| Theme logic | Read/apply/subscribe to `data-theme` and `localStorage` | `src/lib/theme.ts` |\n| Theme hook | React binding for the theme value | `src/lib/useTheme.ts` |\n| Motion preference | Reads `prefers-reduced-motion`, exposes `{ reduced }` | `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts` |\n| Motion provider | Feeds the one preference to `MotionConfig` and react-spring `Globals` | `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx` |\n| Animation barrel | The only public import path for animation | `src/anim/index.ts` |\n| TextReveal | Declarative text reveal wrapper | `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx` |\n| ThemeThumb | Spring-animated thumb for the toggle | `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx` |\n| Tokens (JS) | Spring and duration constants shared by both libraries | `src/anim/tokens.ts` |\n| Design tokens (CSS) | Role colors per theme, derived tokens | `src/styles/tokens.css` |\n| Tailwind entry | `@import 'tailwindcss'`, `@custom-variant dark`, `@theme inline` mapping | `src/styles/index.css` |\n| Base CSS | Resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guards | `src/styles/base.css` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Single-page React app with flat composition. Props flow downward, there is no router and no global state store. Two React contexts exist (motion preference and, via the DOM, theme).\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- Static output only: `npm run build` produces `dist/`; no runtime server or API calls.\n- The DOM is the source of truth for theme: `html[data-theme]` is read by `readTheme()` and written by `applyTheme()` and the pre-paint script in `index.html`.\n- Animation is isolated behind one barrel (`src/anim/index.ts`). Lint rules enforce that sections and layout never import `motion` or `@react-spring/web` directly (`.oxlintrc.json`).\n- Static HTML in `index.html` mirrors the rendered Hero and section headings so content exists before JavaScript runs. `tests/unit/hero-parity.test.tsx` checks the Hero against that markup.\n- Content is inline JSX: copy lives in the components (e.g. `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`). The typed `src/content/` layer described in `docs/architecture.md` does not exist yet.\n\n## Layers\n\n**App shell (`src/app/`):**\n- Purpose: root composition and providers\n- Location: `src/app/`\n- Contains: `App.tsx`, `Providers.tsx`, `Page.tsx`\n- Depends on: `src/anim` (via `Providers`), `src/sections`, `src/components/ui`\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx`\n\n**Sections (`src/sections/`):**\n- Purpose: page content blocks\n- Location: `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx` (Hero) and `src/sections/Stubs/`\n- Contains: section components taking no props today\n- Depends on: `src/layout`, `src/anim` barrel (Hero imports `TextReveal`)\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**Layout (`src/layout/`):**\n- Purpose: shared page structure\n- Location: `src/layout/`\n- Contains: `Section.tsx` (props: `id`, `title`, `className`, `children`), `Container.tsx`\n- Depends on: `src/lib/utils.ts`\n- Used by: sections\n\n**UI components (`src/components/ui/`):**\n- Purpose: reusable interactive pieces\n- Location: `src/components/ui/`\n- Contains: `ThemeToggle.tsx` only. `Button`, `Tag`, `Card`, `Heading`, `Logo`, `Icon` are planned in `docs/architecture.md`.\n- Depends on: `src/anim` barrel, `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/lib/useTheme.ts`\n- Used by: `src/app/App.tsx`, sections (planned)\n\n**Animation (`src/anim/`):**\n- Purpose: the only layer that imports `motion/react` and `@react-spring/web`\n- Location: `src/anim/`\n- Contains: `index.ts` (barrel), `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `tokens.ts`, `declarative/TextReveal.tsx`, `physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`\n- Used by: `src/app/Providers.tsx`, `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`, `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`\n- Must not import: sections, layout, content (enforced by the `**/anim/**` override in `.oxlintrc.json`)\n\n**Lib and hooks (`src/lib/`):**\n- Purpose: framework-agnostic helpers and the theme hook\n- Location: `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/lib/useTheme.ts`, `src/lib/utils.ts`\n- Contains: theme read/apply/subscribe, `cn` class merge helper\n- Used by: components. `src/lib/useTheme.ts` is the React hook for the theme value.\n\n**Styles (`src/styles/`):**\n- Purpose: design tokens and global CSS\n- Location: `src/styles/index.css` (entry, imported by `src/main.tsx`), `tokens.css`, `base.css`\n- Contains: Tailwind v4 config in CSS (`@theme inline`, `@custom-variant dark`), `@source` directives for `index.html` and `src/`\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (page load)\n\n1. Browser loads `index.html`. The inline script in `<head>` reads `localStorage.theme`, falls back to `prefers-color-scheme`, and sets `data-theme` on `<html>` before first paint (`index.html`, lines 9-24).\n2. Vite-built module `src/main.tsx` runs, imports `src/styles/index.css` and the Inter font, and calls `createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(...)` (`src/main.tsx`).\n3. `App` renders `Providers` (which renders `MotionPreferenceProvider`), then the fixed `ThemeToggle` header, then `Page` (`src/app/App.tsx`, `src/app/Providers.tsx`).\n4. `MotionPreferenceProvider` calls `useMotionPreference()`, sets react-spring `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation })`, and wraps children in `MotionConfig` (`src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`).\n5. `Page` renders `<main id=\"main\">` with `Hero` and `SectionStubs` (`src/app/Page.tsx`).\n\n### Theme Toggle Path\n\n1. User clicks the button in `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`. `toggle()` computes the next theme and calls `applyTheme(next)`.\n2. `applyTheme` sets `html[data-theme]` first, so the switch always works, then writes `localStorage` in a try block (`src/lib/theme.ts`).\n3. `useTheme` (`src/lib/useTheme.ts`) observes the attribute via `subscribeTheme`, so React re-renders the `aria-pressed` state.\n4. `ThemeThumb` (`src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`) animates the thumb with react-spring.\n\n### Cross-Tab and OS Theme Sync\n\n1. `subscribeTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts` listens to `storage` events and to `prefers-color-scheme` `change` events.\n2. When no stored choice exists, OS changes are followed. The attribute remains the single source of truth.\n\n### Motion Preference Path\n\n1. `useMotionPreference` (`src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`) queries `(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)`.\n2. `MotionPreferenceProvider` maps that to `MotionConfig reducedMotion` (`'always'` or `'never'`) and to react-spring `skipAnimation`.\n3. Base CSS applies reduced-motion guards (`src/styles/base.css`, `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)`).\n\n**State Management:**\n- No store library. Theme state lives in `html[data-theme]` plus `localStorage`. Motion preference is derived from `matchMedia`. Other UI state is local component state.\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**Animation barrel:**\n- Purpose: one import path for all motion, so libraries can be swapped without touching sections\n- Examples: `src/anim/index.ts`\n- Pattern: re-export of semantic components and hooks. Current exports: `ThemeThumb`, `MotionPreferenceProvider`, `useMotionPreference`, `TextReveal`, `springs`, `durations`, `easeOut`.\n- Usage: `import { TextReveal } from '@/anim'` (see `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`)\n\n**Theme API:**\n- Purpose: one module owns `data-theme` and storage\n- Examples: `src/lib/theme.ts` exports `Theme`, `THEME_STORAGE_KEY`, `readTheme`, `applyTheme`, `subscribeTheme`\n- Pattern: DOM attribute as state, React hook as a subscriber (`src/lib/useTheme.ts`)\n\n**Path alias:**\n- `@/` maps to `src/` in `vite.config.ts`, `vitest.config.ts`, and `tsconfig.app.json` (`paths`)\n- Use `@/sections/Hero/Hero`, not relative `../../` paths across layers\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**Browser entry:**\n- Location: `index.html` (loads `/src/main.tsx` as a module script)\n- Triggers: any page load of the deployed site or `npm run dev`\n- Responsibilities: pre-paint theme script, static fallback markup, module bootstrap\n\n**React root:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: module evaluation\n- Responsibilities: mount `<App />` into `#root`, global CSS import\n\n**Build entry:**\n- Location: `vite.config.ts`\n- Triggers: `npm run build`, `npm run dev`, `npm run preview`\n- Responsibilities: base path from `VITE_BASE`, plugins (`react()`, `tailwindcss()`), `@` alias\n\n**Quality gate entry:**\n- Location: `package.json` `verify` script, invoked by `.github/workflows/ci.yml` and `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`\n- Responsibilities: typecheck, lint, format, unit tests, source gates, build, dist gates\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers in `src/`.\n- **Global state:** `document.documentElement` `data-theme` attribute and `localStorage` key `theme` (`src/lib/theme.ts`). Module-level `Globals` from `@react-spring/web` is mutated by `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`.\n- **Circular imports:** None detected in the current `src/` tree.\n- **Static output:** No server code. Anything served must exist in `public/` or be imported as a module so Vite hashes it.\n- **Base path:** Absolute paths break under `VITE_BASE`. Use `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` (see `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`) or `%BASE_URL%` in `index.html`.\n- **Content rule:** A phone number must never appear in served output. `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` enforces this over `dist/`.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Hard-coded copy in index.html and components\n\n**What happens:** The Hero copy and the mailto link appear in both `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx` and the static markup in `index.html`.\n**Why it's wrong:** Edits to one copy drift from the other. `tests/unit/hero-parity.test.tsx` only catches drift in what it checks.\n**Do this instead:** Change the component and the pre-rendered markup together, and keep `tests/unit/hero-parity.test.tsx` passing.\n\n### Importing animation libraries outside src/anim\n\n**What happens:** Nothing in `src/` does this today. The pattern to avoid is `import { motion } from 'motion/react'` inside `src/sections/` or `src/layout/`.\n**Why it's wrong:** `oxlint` fails the build (`no-restricted-imports` in `.oxlintrc.json`), and the animation barrel is then bypassed.\n**Do this instead:** Add a semantic wrapper in `src/anim/declarative/` or `src/anim/physics/`, export it from `src/anim/index.ts`, then import from `@/anim`.\n\n### Writing theme state without applyTheme\n\n**What happens:** Setting `data-theme` directly, or calling `localStorage.setItem('theme', ...)` from a component.\n**Why it's wrong:** The attribute and the stored value drift, and `subscribeTheme` treats the attribute as the source of truth.\n**Do this instead:** Call `applyTheme(next)` from `src/lib/theme.ts`.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Defensive fallbacks, no thrown errors in the app path.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- `localStorage` access is wrapped in try/catch (`src/lib/theme.ts` `applyTheme`, `storedTheme`; `index.html` inline script). Blocked storage falls back to in-memory choice.\n- `window.matchMedia` access is wrapped in try/catch with a `light` fallback (`src/lib/theme.ts` `systemTheme`, `subscribeTheme`).\n- `document.getElementById('root')!` in `src/main.tsx` assumes the root exists; there is no runtime guard.\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None in app code. Build scripts print to stdout or stderr (`scripts/check-no-phone.mjs`).\n**Validation:** Typecheck via `tsc -b` with `strict`, `noUnusedLocals`, `noUnusedParameters` (`tsconfig.app.json`). Runtime validation of theme values uses `isTheme` (`src/lib/theme.ts`).\n**Authentication:** Not applicable. No user accounts.\n**Accessibility:** Each section is a `<section>` labelled by its heading via `aria-labelledby` (`src/layout/Section.tsx`). The theme toggle exposes `aria-pressed` and `aria-label` (`src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`).\n\n## Planned but Not Yet Present\n\nThese are described in `docs/architecture.md` and do not exist in the repo as of this analysis. Plan against the tracked files, and verify before depending on them:\n- `src/content/` (typed content schema and per-section data modules). Copy currently lives in components.\n- `src/hooks/` (`useActiveSection`, `useMediaQuery`, `useHoverCapable`). The only hook present is `src/lib/useTheme.ts`.\n- `src/components/motion-primitives/` (vendored primitives). `tests/fixtures/motion-primitives/Ok.tsx` exists as a test input.\n- `src/assets/images/` and vite-imagetools image pipeline.\n- Section components beyond Hero (About, Experience, Projects, Skills, Education, Contact). They exist only as stubs.\n- Lenis smooth scroll, lucide-react icons.\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-20*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":5764,"cache_read_input_tokens":83841,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":5764,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01KwiSrAVQ97b4FGQaPaHmF8","agent_id":"a1915f27f75901260","session_id":"06d26458-403d-4f6b-9de8-8af7ecc5ba95","uuid":"c9dbe6cc-76cf-49e6-8322-735b50ae223d","timestamp":"2026-11-20T07:27:31.546Z","request_id":"req_011ChDnRhhywXwXHfZWPVDQr","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Scan codebase tech and architecture"}
